CORPORATE PROFILE
 四環醫藥控股集團有限公司（「四環醫藥」或「本          Founded in 2001 and listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
公司」，連同其附屬公司為「本集團」）    （股份代號：     Limited in 2010, Sihuan Pharmaceutical Holdings Group Ltd. (“Sihuan Pharmaceutical”
 00460.HK）創立於二零零一年，二零一零年於        or the “Company”, together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”) (Stock Code:
 香港聯合交易所有限公司主板上市，是一家以            00460.HK) is an international medical aesthetic and biopharmaceutical company
 創新為引領，堅持創新驅動，擁有獨立領先的            led and driven by innovation, with a leading independent production, and research
 自主生產、研究與開發（「研發」）技術平台，具          and development (“R&D”) technology platform, a rich global product pipeline and a
 備豐富的全球化產品管線和成熟卓越銷售體系            mature and excellent sales system. Focusing on high-growth therapeutic areas such as
 的國際化醫美及生物製藥企業。四環醫藥聚焦            medical aesthetics, oncology, metabolism, diabetes, cardiovascular and cerebrovascular,
 醫 美、腫 瘤、代 謝、糖 尿 病、心 腦 血 管、現 代   modern Chinese medicine and industrial hemp, it adheres to its overall strategic
 中藥及工業大麻等高增長治療領域，一直秉承            objective of “Adhering to the full-speed promotion of a two-wheeled strategy of Sihuan
「堅持全速推進四環醫美及生物製藥雙輪驅動             medical aesthetics and biopharmaceuticals” to build a leading medical aesthetics and
 戰略」的整體戰略目標來打造中國領先的醫美            biopharmaceutical enterprise in China.
 及生物製藥企業。

MANAGEMENT DISCUSSION AND ANALYSIS


行業概況                                       INDUSTRY OVERVIEW
中國醫藥行業正在發生深刻變化，二零二四年                       China’s pharmaceutical industry is experiencing significant transformations. Despite
上半年，醫藥經濟仍處於復蘇中，投資退潮、                       being in a phase of recovery in the first half of 2024, the pharmaceutical industry is
增長承壓等挑戰不容忽視；與此同時，政策端                       encountering challenges such as investment pullbacks and growth limitations that should
暖風頻吹，創新升級、消費復蘇等成為行業增                       not be underestimated. Meanwhile, the positive policy development, innovation and
長的新動能，高質量發展開始加速。新需求推                       upgrading, consumer recovery and more are serving as new drivers for industry growth,
動新技術的產生，新技術的迭代升級加速行業                       hastening its shift towards high-quality development. New demands are fostering
效率、效能的提升，由此形成的新質生產力催                       the creation of new technologies, while the iterative upgrading of new technologies
生各種新業態。                                    accelerates the improvement of industry efficiency and effectiveness. Consequently, the
                                           new productivities gives rise to a variety of new industries.


二零二四年，我國生物醫藥產業正進入高質量                       In 2024, China’s biopharmaceutical industry is embarking on a new phase of high-quality
發 展 的 新 階 段，國 內 創 新 藥 實 現 了 從「鼓 勵 大        development. The domestic innovative pharmaceuticals industry has achieved a leap from
規模創新」到「鼓勵高質量創新」的跨越。在以                      “encouraging large-scale innovation” to “encouraging high-quality innovation”. Under
創新為導向的政策影響下，隨著部分創新藥及                       the influence of innovation-oriented policies, some high-quality companies specializing
仿創結合的優質公司已逐步邁入收穫期，在二                       in innovative drugs and generic-innovative drug combinations have gradually begun
零二四年內將迎來密集催化，具有差異化優勢、                      to enter a phase of fruition. The year 2024 is positioned for notable catalytic strides,
臨床價值突出、合規商業化能力強或具備平台                       with innovative drug companies possessing differentiated advantages, exceptional
能力的創新藥企業有望成為行業增長的主要動                       clinical value, strong commercialization compliance, or robust platform capability which
力。與此同時，企業的「走出去」與「引進來」，                     are expected to become the main driving force for industry growth. Meanwhile, the
也將進一步激發醫藥經濟活力。                             “going out” and “bringing in” of enterprises will further stimulate the vitality of the
                                           pharmaceutical economy.


國家不斷出台醫藥產業政策與配套措施，鼓勵                       The Chinese government has consistently issued policies and supportive measures for
醫藥行業健康發展。政策發佈密集度和力度均                       the pharmaceutical industry to foster its healthy growth. The intensity and robustness
超過往，通過醫藥、醫保、醫療政策的聯動倒                       of these policies have surpassed those of previous years, compelling the pharmaceutical
逼醫藥產業進行轉型升級，朝著創新驅動、高                       industry to transform and upgrade through the integration of pharmaceutical, health
質量增長驅動的方向發展。醫改進入快通道，                       insurance and medical service policies. This shift aims to drive development towards
醫療保障制度更加成熟。                                innovation-driven and high-quality growth. The healthcare reform has accelerated,
                                           pushing the healthcare insurance system towards greater maturity.

醫藥創新供給端改革持續推進的同時，支付端                    As the reform in drug innovation supply progresses, the payment sector is enhancing its
對於創新藥的支持力度也在加大。創新藥的醫                    support for innovative drugs. The approval timelines of National Reimbursement Drug List
保准入週期越來越短，談判規則不斷完善優化。                   for innovative drugs are becoming shorter, and the negotiation protocols are constantly
二零二三年十二月十三日，新版國家醫保目錄                    being refined. On 13 December 2023, a new edition of the National Reimbursement
公佈。此次醫保談判有 25 個創新藥參加，談成                 Drug List was announced, with 25 innovative drugs participated in negotiations, 23 of
23 個，成功率高達 92%。本次調整中，57 個品              which were successfully negotiated, resulting in a success rate of 92%. The adjustment
種實現了當年獲批、當年納入醫保目錄。目前，                   shows 57 varieties were approved and added to the drug list within the same year.
80% 的新藥能夠在上市後兩年內進入醫保目錄。                 Currently, 80% of new drugs can be included in the drug list within two years after their
                                        launch.

在國家「創新驅動」戰略引領下，受人口基數與                   Guided by the national “innovation-driven” strategy and impacted by the population
老齡化的驅動，中國醫藥市場有著強勁的底層                    and aging, there are substantial underlying demands in China’s pharmaceutical
需求，過去三年醫藥市場的銷售渠道更為分散，                   market. Over the past three years, the pharmaceutical market has experienced
驅動商業模式更加多元化，給中國醫藥企業的                    increased fragmentation across sales channels and a diversification of business models,
銷售與增長帶來了更多的機遇與更大的挑戰。                    offering more opportunities and greater challenges to the sales and expansion of
受益於國家「騰籠換鳥」的策略，醫藥市場未來                   China’s pharmaceutical companies. Empowered by the nation’s “change cage for
的增速將以創新藥為主導，醫藥產業加速進入                    birds” strategy, the growth speed of the pharmaceutical market in the future will be
大破大立的新週期，優勝劣汰下的整合、集中                    dominated by innovative drugs. The pharmaceutical industry has entered into a new
度提升、創新破卷等一系列趨勢也開始加速確                    phase marked by significant breakthroughs. A series of trends, such as consolidation,
立，同時也拉開了產業成長的新篇章。                       increased concentration, and innovation through the process of survival of the strong
                                        and elimination of the weak, have also begun to accelerate, ushering in a new chapter
                                        of growth for the industry at the same time.

二零二四年是推動消費從企穩恢復轉向持續擴                    2024 marks a transition where consumption shifts from a gradual rebound to
大的一年，也是醫美行業提質升級的關鍵一年。                   sustainable growth. It is also a critical year for the medical aesthetic industry as it strives
根據《中國醫美行業二零二四年度洞悉報告》，                   to enhance its quality standards. According to the “China Medical Aesthetic Industry
隨著服務型消費的逐步修復，醫美滲透率的不                    Outlook Report 2024”, with the gradual resumption of service-oriented consumption,
斷提升和多元化品質醫美需求的驅動，預計中                    driven by the rising penetration rate of medical aesthetics and growing demands for
國 醫 美 市 場 增 速 可 在 未 來 幾 年 內 保 持 10% 至   diverse and high-quality medical aesthetics services, it is expected that the growth rate
15% 的增長。且隨著醫美需求側趨向理性，求                  of 10% to 15% in China’s medical aesthetics market over the coming years. Given the
美者在追求性價比醫美的同時，更追求優質安                    rationality behind the demand for medical aesthetics, aesthetics seekers are not only
全與個性化兼具品質醫美服務和治療體驗，在                    pursuing cost-effective services but also prioritizing the quality, safety and personalized
這樣的需求下，具備豐富高品質產品、能提供                    quality of medical aesthetic services and treatment experiences. Under such demands,
高質強服務的頭部企業將會持續受益。                       leading enterprises equipped with a diverse range of top-notch products and the ability
                                        to provide high-quality and exceptional services will continue to benefit.

因此未來也一定為一直以來相信本集團和支持                      to withstand the cyclical risks of the industry will also be significantly increased in the

庫務政策                                       Treasury policy
本集團主要以自有內部資源為其日常經營業務                       The Group finances its ordinary operations mainly with internally generated resources.
提供所需資金。本集團資本管理的主要目標為                       The principal objective of the Group’s capital management is to maintain its ability to
保持按持續基準經營之能力。本集團定期審閱                       operate on a continuous basis. The Group regularly reviews its capital structure to ensure
其資本架構，以確保本集團的財務資源足以支                       that the Group has sufficient financial resources to support its business operations.
撐其業務營運。

人力資源及僱員薪金                                 Human resources and remuneration of employees
人力資源是本集團在充滿挑戰的環境中得以成                      Human resources are indispensable assets to the Group’s success in a competitive
功的不可或缺資產。本集團致力為全體僱員提                      environment. The Group is committed to providing competitive remuneration packages
供具競爭力的薪酬待遇，定期檢討人力資源政                      to all the employees and regularly reviewing human resources policies, to encourage
策，以鼓勵僱員努力提升本公司價值及促進本                      employees to work towards enhancing the value of the Company and promoting the
公司的可持續增長。本集團亦已採納購股權計                      sustainable growth of the Company. The Group has also adopted share option scheme
劃及股份獎勵計劃，以表揚及獎勵員工對本集                      and share award scheme to recognise and reward the contribution of the employees for
團之營運及未來發展作出的貢獻。                           the benefit of the Group’s operations and future development.

的培訓工作，促進本集團人才的培育與發展，                      positions at different levels to promote the cultivation and development of talents in the
保障各類人才的持續供給。                              Group and ensure continuous supply of various types of talents.

閱中期財務資料包括主要向負責財務和會計事                      Standards Board. A review of inter im financial information consists of making inquiries,
務的人員作出詢問，及進行分析性和其他審閱                      primarily of persons responsible for financial and accounting matters, and applying
程序。審閱範圍遠少於根據國際核數準則進行                      analytical and other review procedures. A review is substantially less in scope than
審核的範圍，故我們無法保證我們將知悉在審                      an audit conducted in accordance with International Standards on Auditing and
核中可能被發現的所有重大事項。因此，我們                      consequently does not enable us to obtain assurance that we would become aware of all

1.    公 司 及 集 團資料                               1.   CORPORATE AND GROUP INFORMATION
      四環醫藥控股集團有限公司（「本公司」）                            SIHUAN PHARMACEUTICAL HOLDINGS GROUP LTD. (the “Company”) was
      根據百慕達公司法於百慕達註冊成立為                              incorporated in Bermuda under the Bermuda Companies Act as an exempted
      獲豁免公司。                                         company.


      本 公 司 為 投 資 控 股 公 司。本 公 司 及 其 附                The Company is an investment holding company. The principal activities of the
      屬公司（統稱「本集團」）的主要業務為於                            Company and its subsidiaries (together, the “Group”) are the research and
      中華人民共和國（「中國」）研究及開發                             development (“R&D”), manufacture and sale of pharmaceutical and medical
     （「研 發」）、以 及 製 造 及 銷 售 醫 藥 及 醫 美                 aesthetic products in the People’s Republic of China (the “PRC”).
      產品。

      House, 2 Church Street, Hamilton HM 11,        Street, Hamilton HM 11, Bermuda. The address of the principal place of business
      Bermuda。本 集 團 香 港 主 要 營 業 地 點 為                of the Group in Hong Kong is Room 4905, Office Tower, Convention Plaza, 1

20. 金 融 工 具 的公平值及公平                          20. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY OF
    值 層 級（續）                                     FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (continued)
     按公平值計入損益的非上市股權投資的                              The fair values of unlisted equity investments designated at fair value through
     公 平 值，乃 根 據 並 非 由 可 見 市 價 或 比 率                profit or loss have been estimated using a market-based valuation technique
     支 持 之 假 設，使 用 市 場 估 值 方 法 預 測。                 based on assumptions that are not supported by observable market prices or rates.
     估 值 要 求 董 事 根 據 行 業、規 模、槓 桿 及                  The valuation requires the directors to determine comparable public companies
     戰 略 釐 定 可 資 比 較 公 眾 公 司，並 就 識 別                based on industry, size, leverage and strategy, and to calculate an appropriate
     出的各可資比較公司計算合適價格倍數，                             price multiple, such as enterprise value to earnings before interest, taxes,
     例 如 企 業 價 值 對 除 利 息、稅 項、折 舊 及                  depreciation and amortisation (“EV/EBITDA”) multiple and price to earnings (“P/E”)
     攤銷前盈利（「EV/EBITDA」）倍數及價格對                       multiple, for each comparable company identified. The multiple is calculated by
     盈利（「P/E」）倍數。倍數乃以可資比較公                          dividing the enterprise value of the comparable company by an earnings measure.
     司的企業價值除以盈利衡量計算得出。                              The trading multiple is then discounted for considerations such as illiquidity and
     交易倍數以可資比較公司根據公司獨有                              size differences between the comparable companies based on company-specific
     因 素 及 情 況 的 考 慮 因 素，例 如 非 流 通 性                facts and circumstances. The discounted multiple is applied to the corresponding
     及 規 模 差 異，予 以 貼 現。貼 現 倍 數 用 於                  earnings measure of the unlisted equity investments to measure the fair value.
     非上市股權投資的相應盈利衡量以計量                              The directors believe that the estimated fair values resulting from the valuation
     公 平 值。董 事 認 為 估 值 方 法 得 出 的 估 計                technique, which are recorded in the consolidated statement of financial position,
     公平值（記錄於綜合財務狀況表）及相關                             and the related changes in fair values, which are recorded in the consolidated
     公平值變動（記錄於綜合損益及其他全面                             statement of profit or loss and other comprehensive income, are reasonable, and
     收益表）屬合理，並為報告期末最合適的                             that they were the most appropriate values at the end of the reporting period.
     價值。

     本 集 團 投 資 於 非 上 市 投 資，即 中 國 內 地                The Group invests in unlisted investments, which represent wealth management
     銀 行 發 行 的 理 財 產 品。本 集 團 已 使 用 貼                products issued by banks in Chinese Mainland. The Group has estimated the fair
     現 現 金 流 量 估 值 模 型，根 據 類 似 年 期 及                value of these unlisted investments by using a discounted cash flow valuation
     風險的工具的市場利率估算該等非上市                              model based on the market interest rates of instruments with similar terms and
     投資的公平值。                                        risks.

     對於按公平值計入其他全面收益的非上                              For the fair value of the unlisted equity investments at fair value through other
     市 股 權 投 資 的 公 平 值，管 理 層 已 估 計 使                comprehensive income, management has estimated the potential effect of using
     用合理可行的替代方法作為估值模型參                              reasonably possible alternatives as inputs to the valuation model.
     數的潛在影響。
